We need compute h from weights. We can compute full c=q? q=n*c. To compute h = T_w p as above. Implementation:
def convolve_self_scaled(p): returns c length 2n-1 via FFT.
def weighted_overlap(p, w):
    n = len(p); Lc=2*n-1
    # wr = w[Lc-1::-1]
    size = 1
    while size < (Lc-1)+n: size*=2  # 3n-2
    Fw = np.fft.rfft(wr, size)
    Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, size)
    q = np.fft.irfft(Fw*Fp, size)
    # h_i = q[Lc-1 - i]

### Core functions
- `clean_nonnegative(a, target_sum=1.0)`: np.asarray float; a=np.nan_to_num; clip(0,1000); if sum<1e-12 uniform; a/=a.sum()*target_sum? Actually target=1 for p; if target n, scale. For our p, sum=1. Return.
- `fft_full_conv(a)`: as above, returns c[:2n-1] real, max(0).
- `fast_score_scaled(c,n)`: `2.0 * n * float(np.max(c))`. Since p sum1.
- `fast_score(a)`: c=fft_full_conv(a); return 2n maxc.
- `softmax_weights(q, kappa)`: maxq=np.max(q); w=np.exp((q-maxq)/kappa); s=w.sum(); if s==0 uniform; return w/s, maxq.
- `weighted_overlap(p,w)`: as above.
- `step_mle(p, w, beta)`: Use hn = weighted_overlap / mean; factor = np.exp(-beta*(hn-1)); factor=np.clip(factor, 1e-12, 1e12); np = p*factor; normalize.
But if p has zeros, factor>0 makes previously zero positive. That's okay; maybe fill support. But if support should be sparse? Our dense solution has no zeros. Starting from parent might have zeros? If partner high, h_i high, factor small for i, so decrease i (the opposite of high partner). This increases partner? After normalization, decreasing i increases relative partner. So for pair, if p_left < p_right, h_left = p_right high => decrease left; h_right=p_left low => increase right; thus amplifies asymmetry (rich get richer) toward more asymmetry. Is that desirable? ### Optimizer function
Pseudo:
```
def optimize_one(p0, max_time, seed, min_time=2, label=""):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    p = clean(p0)
    n = len(p)
    # initial exact eval
    s0 = float(evaluate_sequence(p.tolist()))
    best_exact = s0 if finite else inf
    best_exact_p = p.copy()
    c = fft_full_conv(p)
    maxq = n*max(c)
    cur_score = 2*maxq
    best_fast = cur_score
    best_fast_p = p.copy()
    beta = 0.8 # initial?
    kappa = max(0.02, 0.6) # maybe
    no_improve = 0
    time_start = time.monotonic()
    time_max = time_start + max_time
    while time < time_max:
        # schedule based on elapsed fraction
        frac = (time-time_start)/(max_time or 1)
        # kappa from 0.4 to 0.03
        kappa = 0.05 + 0.35*(1-frac) + 0.30? actually max 0.35 min0.05? Let's choose 0.03 + 0.4*(1-frac) maybe.
        # beta maybe start0.7 decay to 0.05? Or adapt.
        # Compute gradient using c already current? At end of loop set c.
        q = n*c
        maxq = float(np.max(q))
        # if cur_score not finite? reinitialize from best_fast
        if not finite: p=best_fast_p; c=fft; ...
        w, maxq = softmax_weights(q, kappa)
        hn = weighted_overlap(p,w)
        mu = hn.mean()
        if not finite or mu <= 0: p = best_fast_p; continue
        hn /= mu
        # Adapt beta to dev scale:
        dev = hn - 1.0
        sd = float(np.std(dev))
        # Choose beta such that beta*sd ~ target (e.g., 0.3) if sd not tiny.
        # But if current beta good, adjust gradually.
        if sd > 1e-8:
            target = 0.25 + 0.5*(1-frac) # early 0.75, late 0.25
            ideal = target / sd
            # combine with current beta: beta = 0.5*beta + 0.5*clip(ideal,0.01,3)
            beta = 0.7*beta + 0.3*np.clip(ideal, 0.01, 3.0)
        # Line search with temperature
        temperature = 0.005*(1-frac) + 1e-6 # score units
        accepted=False
        for attempt in range(4):
            factor = np.exp(-beta * np.clip(hn - 1.0, -10, 20)) # decide ranges
            factor = np.clip(factor, 1e-12, 1e12)
            np = p * factor
            np = clean(np, 1.0)
            # Fast score
            c_new = fft_full_conv(np)
            s_new = 2.0 * n * float(np.max(c_new))
            # accept if improves best_fast or better than cur+temp
            if s_new < best_fast - 1e-12 or s_new < cur_score + temperature:
                 # If s_new < best_fast, update best_fast
                 if s_new < best_fast:
                     best_fast = s_new; best_fast_p = np.copy(); no_improve=0
                 p = np; c = c_new; cur_score=s_new; accepted=True
                 break
            else:
                 beta *= 0.5
        if not accepted:
            cur_score = 2*n*np.max(c) # no change
        # exact eval periodically or if best_fast improved
        # exact every e.g. 1s or 50 iters if enough
        if time since last_exact > 1.0 or (accepted and it%20==0): evaluate_sequence(p.tolist()) exact, update best_exact if lower.
        # If no_improve > threshold, perturb from best_fast or random
        no_improve += 1 if not accepted else 0
        if no_improve > 200:
            # perturb best
            p = perturb(best_fast_p, rng, sigma=0.15)
            c=fft_full_conv(p); cur_score=...
            beta = max(beta,0.3)
            no_improve=0
            # Maybe exact not needed
        it +=1
    # final exact evaluate best_fast_p and best_exact p
    return best_exact_p, best_exact
```

#### Continuous / finite difference ideas
Could also solve a "dual" multiplicative update directly for λ and p? Let's derive a two-variable algorithm:
We want minimize max c. Consider constraints c_k ≤M. For given lags k, weights λ. Multiplicative p update flattens Tλp. We can update λ to focus on peaks: λ_k ∝ exp(c_k/κ). That's what we do. This is like self-consistent.

Could add "average coefficient" constraint? Since sum q fixed, no.

Maybe use **entropy maximization** under peak constraints: Given a candidate peak M, maximize entropy -∑p log p subject to c_k≤M, p≥0, sum1. If feasible for target M, then score ≤2M. This is convex? Constraints c_k≤M are nonconvex. But can use barrier: maximize entropy - penalty ∑ softmax? Maybe.

What if we directly optimize a polynomial in Fourier domain? Let p_i = e^{θ_i}/Z. For random smooth θ, coefficients p. We can do gradient descent on θ with softmax objective; positivity guaranteed. This avoids projection and zeros. Since p_i positive smooth. We can represent θ on grid. The gradient of logsumexp(q) wrt θ: ∂p_i = p_i(δ_{ij} - p_j); ∂F/∂θ_i = ∑_j ∂F/∂p_j ∂p_j/∂θ_i = ∂F_i - p_i∑∂F_j. If ∂F_j = (2/κ)T_w p_j, then ∂F/∂θ_i = (2/κ)[h_i - p_i ∑ h_j? Wait ∑ ∂F_j p_j? For exponential normalization, ∂p_j/∂θ_i = p_i(δ_{ij} - p_j). Sum_j ∂F_j(δ_{ij}-p_j)=∂F_i - p_i ∑_j ∂F_j. If ∂F_j proportional h_j, sum h_j fixed? Not necessarily. This is like projected gradient in log space. Multiplicative update p_i *= exp(-β(h_i - mean h)) with mean over i? The exact gradient with sum constraint uses ∑ p_j ∂F_j, not mean h. Our factor uses mean(h) over i. Which is correct? Let's check: F(p) fixed sum. Projected gradient direction in log-simplex exponential family: p_i' ∝ p_i exp(-η ∂F/∂p_i). This uses raw gradient, no subtract mean (normalization handles sum). The normalization subtracts log(sum exp) equivalent to subtract mean of log? The factor before normalization is exp(-η g_i). After normalization, relative factor is exp(-η(g_i - 1/n∑? no, normalization constant independent of i: exp(-ηg_i)/∑pexp(-ηg)). Thus relative p_i factor = exp(-η(g_i - g_bar_geom? Actually ratio p_i'/p_i = exp(-η g_i)/weighted average? Not exactly using same p weights? If p not uniform for weights, the sum in denominator =∑p_j exp(-ηg_j), not arithmetic mean. But subtracting mean(h) (arithmetic) is heuristic. For small η, p_i'/p_i ≈1 - ηg_i + η∑p_jg_j (because denominator derivative). That subtracts dot(p,g), not arithmetic mean. We used mean h over all i. Should use weighted mean `mu = sum(p*h)`? Let's derive for multiplicative P utility: To perform a scaled gradient step in simplex while preserving sum, for small η: p_i' = p_i - η(g_i - ∑p_j g_j) (projection along tangent). Relative factor ≈1 - η(g_i - weighted mean). So use `mu_weighted = dot(p, g)`, not arithmetic mean. In our update h, g=(2/κ)h. So weighted mean m = ∑p_i h_i. This is not mean(h) but ∑p h. Let's compute: For central peak, h_i=p_{n-i}; m=∑p_i p_{n-i}=c_center, the active peak! This is meaningful. The logsumexp gradient's denominator weighted mean =∑p_j ∂F_j = (2/κ)∑p_j h_j =2 M? For one peak, h_i=p_{k-i}, ∑p_i h_i = c_k = peak. So update factor exp(-β(h_i / c_k?)) if we normalize by weighted mean. That makes hn = h / weighted_mean, mean weighted? ∑p hn=1. For uniform central peak, h_i=1/n, c=1/n, hn=1. Good. For pair with p_left<p_right, h_left=p_right, h_right=p_left; weighted mean c=p_left p_right. hn_left = p_right/c =1/p_left (since c=p_left p_right), hn_right=1/p_right. If p_left< p_right, hn_left > hn_right. Factor decreases left more. This amplifies asymmetry strongly. This may be better than arithmetic mean.

For general weights, weighted mean `m = dot(p, h) = ∑_k w_k c_k`? Let's check: h_i =∑_k w_k p_{k-i}. ∑_i p_i h_i =∑_k w_k ∑_i p_i p_{k-i} =∑_k w_k c_k. Yes. This is the weighted average convolution coefficient. Normalizing hn = h / m, the weighted average w.r.t p is 1. This exactly matches the linearization of normalized gradient step in log-simplex (if p normalized sum1). So use weighted mean, not arithmetic.

Thus `mu = np.dot(p, hn_raw)`; if mu <=0 fallback. hn = hn_raw / mu. Then update factor = exp(-β*(hn - 1)). This is more mathematically faithful. Good.

Resampling parent to n:
```
def resample_to_length(a, n):
    if len(a)==n: return a
    if len(a)<2: return uniform
    xp = np.linspace(0, len(a)-1, len(a))
    xq = np.linspace(0, len(a)-1, n)
    return np.interp(xq, xp, a)
```

### Optimizer detailed code
Let's write robust top-level functions.

```
def clean_sequence(a, target_sum=1.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0: return None
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = a.sum()
    if s < 1e-15:
       a = np.ones_like(a)
       s = a.sum()
    a = a / s * target_sum
    # cap maybe 1000? target 1 so not needed
    return a
```

FFT size power of two:
```
def next_pow2(x):
    p=1
    while p < x: p <<= 1
    return p
```
Could precompute size in optimize.

`fft_convolution_pair(a, size, n)`: Not a helper? We'll write inline.

Top-level `fft_self_conv(a)` for general use (fast_score, shape): size next_pow2(2*n-1), `fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size); c=...` Return c.

`fast_score_from_c(c,n): return 2.0*n*float(c.max()) if c.size else inf`.

`fast_score(a): c=fft_self_conv(a); return 2.0*len(a)*float(np.max(c))`.

In optimize, for efficiency use size3. Need a function to compute c and Fp:
Top-level `compute_fp_and_conv(p, size, n)` returns Fp, c. But top-level okay.
```
def compute_fp_conv(p, size, n):
    Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(Fp*Fp, size)[:2*n-1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return Fp, c
```

`weighted_overlap_from_fp(w, Fp, size, n)` returns h.
```
def weighted_overlap_from_fp(w, Fp, size, n):
    Lc=2*n-1
    wr = w[Lc-1:0:-1]
    Fw = np.fft.rfft(wr, size)
    h_full = np.fft.irfft(Fw * Fp, size)
    h = h_full[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    return h
```
Need ensure h nonnegative? It may have tiny negatives due FFT; set `np.maximum(h,0)`? Weighted overlap should be nonnegative; numerical errors can yield -1e-16. Clip to 0 for stability.

Softmax weights:
```
def softmax_weights(q, kappa):
    maxq = float(np.max(q))
    # If kappa very small, use top peaks maybe to avoid zero weights? 
    z = np.exp((q - maxq) / kappa)
    s = z.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not finite: z = np.zeros_like(q); idx=np.argmax(q); z[idx]=1
    else: z /= s
    return z, maxq
```
For very small κ, exponent underflows; fine. If q-max negative large, zeros.

Line search function top-level:
```
def trial_update(p, hn, beta, size, n):
    # hn normalized weighted mean approx1? maybe not if clipping; but still
    factor = np.exp(-beta * np.clip(hn - 1.0, -8.0, 12.0))  # choose bounds
    factor = np.maximum(factor, 1e-300)
    np2 = p * factor
    s = np2.sum()
    if s <=0: return None, None
    np2 /= s
    Fp = np.fft.rfft(np2, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(Fp*Fp, size)[:2*n-1]
    c = np.maximum(c,0)
    score = 2.0*n*float(c.max())
    return np2, c, Fp, score
```
But returning many. Top-level okay. However this computes c for each trial. In optimize, we already have hn from current p,w. Good.

Better: Factor with exp can be very small or large. If hn-1 large positive, factor tiny; if large negative, factor huge. Clip dev to [-5/beta?]. Dynamic: Use `dev = hn - 1.0`; `dev = np.clip(dev, -1.0/beta, 3.0)`? Wait if beta=0.2, -1/beta=-5; factor min exp(1)=2.7? Hmm. We want prevent factor >10 and <0.01. Use after exp: factor=np.clip(factor, 1e-3, 1e3). This allows factor up to1000, but normalization. Good.

Actually for small beta, dev negative maybe -0.1 factor 1.1; fine. Clip exp range [-500,500].

Adaptive beta:
```
# Weighted mean already 1, so np.dot(p,hn) =1 if no clip.
dev = hn - 1.0
# weighted std
var = float(np.dot(p, dev*dev))
sd = sqrt(var)
# choose ideal beta: aim beta*sd=0.35 early, 0.15 late
target = 0.35*(1-frac)*0.8+0.15? 
ideal = target/sd if sd>1e-6 else beta

#### `clean_sequence`
```
def clean_sequence(a, target_sum=1.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    # cap at 1000 for safety
    a = np.minimum(a, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(a.shape, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(a.sum())
    a = (a / s) * target_sum
    # Ensure finite
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
       return np.full_like(a, target_sum / max(1,a.size))
    a = a / s * target_sum
    return a
```
Target sum often 1. If target_sum=1, values small. Capping 1000 before normalization irrelevant because if one value 1000 and others0, after normalize sum1 could still 1 (<1000). fine.

Need maybe avoid too large values causing exact? We sum1, max <=1, under 1000. good.

#### `fft_self_conv`
```
def fft_self_conv(a):
    n = len(a)
    if n <= 1: return a*a.copy()
    size=1
    while size < 2*n-1: size <<=1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, size)[:2*n-1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c
```
If a has small negative due numeric? We pass nonnegative.

#### `fast_score`
```
def fast_score(a):
    c = fft_self_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0: return np.inf
    return 2.0 * len(a) * float(np.max(c))
```

#### `upsample_sequence`
```
def upsample_sequence(a, n):
    a = clean_sequence(a, 1.0)
    if a.size == n: return a
    if a.size < 2: return np.full(n, 1.0/n)
    xp = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    xq = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    b = np.interp(xq, xp, a)
    return clean_sequence(b, 1.0)
```

#### `ramp_density`
```
def ramp_density(n, alpha=0.5, seed=0):
    t = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    # maybe exp(alpha*(2t-1))
    p = np.exp(alpha*(2.0*t -1.0))
    return clean_sequence(p,1.0)
```
This yields exponential ramp; for alpha up to 2.

#### `random_smooth_density`
```
def random_smooth_density(n, seed=0, strength=1.0, low_modes=8):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    theta = rng.standard_normal(n)
    F = np.fft.rfft(theta)
    keep = min(len(F), low_modes+1)
    F[keep:] = 0.0
    s = np.fft.irfft(F, n)
    s = s - s.mean()
    sd = float(np.sqrt((s*s).mean()))
    if sd < 1e-12:
        s = rng.standard_normal(n)
        s = s - s.mean()
        sd = float(np.sqrt((s*s).mean()))
    p = np.exp(strength * s / sd)
    return clean_sequence(p,1.0)
```
For n maybe 4096, low_modes up to 8. Could create smooth undulations. Maybe multiple.

#### `shape_objective`
```
def shape_objective(v, n_low, M):
    try:
        raw = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
        if raw.size != M or not np.isfinite(raw).all():
            return 10.0
        raw = np.clip(raw, -6.0, 6.0)
        vals = np.exp(raw)
        if vals.sum() <=0: return 10.0
        xk = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, M)
        xq = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_low)
        dens = np.interp(xq, xk, vals)
        dens = np.maximum(dens, 0.0)
        s = dens.sum()
        if s <=0: return 10.0
        dens /= s
        c = fft_self_conv(dens)
        return float(2.0 * n_low * np.max(c))
    except Exception:
        return 10.0
```
Return 10 if bad. But `xk` and `xq` recomputed each eval; shape_search calls many. Could pass precomputed? To avoid closures, top-level can use global? But M/n fixed; recomputing small okay (512). For M=16, n=512, interp cheap. Could optimize by precomputing in a tuple via args: `shape_objective(v, n_low, M, xk, xq)`; SciPy fun args. Let's do for speed:
```
def shape_objective(v, n_low, M, xk, xq): ...
```
Then start `minimize(shape_objective, x0, args=(n_low, M, xk, xq), ...)`.

#### `shape_search`
```
def shape_search(n_low, M, seed=1, time_limit=60.0):
    global _SP_MINIMIZE
    t0=time.monotonic()
    if not HAVE_SCIPY: return None
    xk = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,M)
    xq = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n_low)
    starts=[]
    starts.append(np.zeros(M))
    starts.append(np.linspace(-1.5, 1.5, M))
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    starts.append(rng.normal(0,1.0,M)*0.8)
    best_v=None; best_score=np.inf
    for x0 in starts:
        if time.monotonic()-t0 > time_limit: break
        try:
            res = _SP_MINIMIZE(shape_objective, x0, args=(n_low, M, xk, xq), method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter': 1200, 'xatol':0.05, 'fatol':1e-7, 'adaptive': True})
        except Exception:
            try: res = _SP_MINIMIZE(...)
        if res is not None and np.isfinite(res.fun):
            sens = res.x
            sens = np.clip(sens, -6,6)
            s = shape_objective(sens, n_low, M, xk, xq)
            if s < best_score:
                best_score=s; best_v=sens.copy()
        # Also evaluate x0 maybe
        s0=...
    # Maybe random local perturbations of best_v
    if best_v is not None:
        for _ in range(20):
            v = best_v + rng.normal(0,0.2,M)
            v=np.clip(v,-6,6)
            s=shape_objective(...)
            if s<best_score: ...
    if best_v is None: return None
    # Upsample
    dens low? We need p_low for upsample. Convert:
    vals=np.exp(np.clip(best_v,-6,6)); dens=np.interp(xq,xk,vals); return clean_sequence(dens,1.0)
```

#### `compute_fp_conv`
```
def compute_fp_conv(p, size, n):
    Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(Fp*Fp, size)[:2*n-1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return Fp, c
```
Use size maybe next_pow2(3n-2); for c length 2n-1, okay. If n=1, size? handle.

#### `weighted_overlap_from_fp`
```
def weighted_overlap_from_fp(w, Fp, size, n):
    Lc=2*n-1
    # Need reverse w of length Lc
    wr = w[Lc-1:0:-1]
    Fw = np.fft.rfft(wr, size)
    h_full = np.fft.irfft(Fw * Fp, size)
    h = h_full[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
    return h
```
Check slice: length 2n-1, indices n-1 to 2n-2 inclusive length n. `h_full[n-1:2*n-1]` yields n elements ascending [i=n-1,... i=0? Actually h_full[n-1] corresponds i=n-1, h_full[2n-2] i=0. Reverse -> i=0..n-1. Good.

If n=1, Lc=1, wr length1, h_full[0:1][::-1] ok. size next_pow2(1?) For optimize n=1 unlikely.

#### `softmax_weights`
Use q length Lc. For q maybe c*n, max. If kappa <=0? set.
```
def softmax_weights(q, kappa):
    if kappa <= 1e-12:
       w=np.zeros_like(q); idx=np.argmax(q); w[idx]=1; return w, float(q[idx])
    maxq=float(np.max(q))
    # To avoid overflow, exp((q-maxq)/kappa)
    e = np.exp((q - maxq)/kappa)
    s=float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <=0:
       w=np.zeros_like(q); idx=int(np.argmax(q)); w[idx]=1.0
    else:
       w=e/s
    return w, maxq
```
q finite. If all q zero (p sum small) not.

#### `trial_update`
Top-level, but for speed, inline in optimize? We can make top-level:
```
def trial_update(p, hn, beta, size, n):
    dev = hn - 1.0
    # clip dev to prevent extreme exponent
    dev = np.clip(dev, -50.0, 50.0)
    factor = np.exp(-beta * dev)
    factor = np.clip(factor, 1e-12, 1e12)
    np2 = p * factor
    s = np2.sum()
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s): return None
    np2 = np2/s
    Fp2 = np.fft.rfft(np2, size)
    c2 = np.fft.irfft(Fp2*Fp2, size)[:2*n-1]
    c2 = np.maximum(c2,0.0)
    score2 = 2.0*n*float(np.max(c2))
    return np2, c2, Fp2, score2
```
But dev clip independent of beta. If beta small, factor near1; if beta large, clip. Good. Need maybe factor can be >1e12 for dev=-50 beta=0.001; normalized but huge values maybe overflow? p small, but clip 1e12. Good. Could set factor clip [1e-100, 1e100].

However computing rfft for each trial uses size from optimize. Good.

Pseudo code robust:
```
def optimize_one(p0, seed=0, time_limit=60.0, external_best_p=None, label=""):
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+max(1.0,time_limit)
    p = clean_sequence(p0,1.0)
    n=p.size
    size=1
    while size < 3*n - 2: size <<=1
    if n == 1: ... exact eval return
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # init Fp,c
    Fp,c = compute_fp_conv(p,size,n)
    cur_score = 2*n*c.max()
    best_fast = cur_score; best_fast_p = p.copy()
    # exact init
    best_exact = np.inf; best_exact_p = p.copy()
    if time_limit > 0.5:
        try:
            ex = float(evaluate_sequence(best_fast_p.tolist()))
            if np.isfinite(ex): best_exact=ex; best_exact_p=best_fast_p.copy()
        except: pass
    if external_best_p is not None and len == n:
        ebp=clean_sequence(external_best_p,1.0)
        # fast and exact maybe
        f=fast_score(ebp) # uses separate fft, okay
        if f < best_fast: best_fast=f; best_fast_p=ebp.copy()
        # exact evaluate extern if time
        try: ex=...
    # kappa/beta schedule
    beta=0.6
    kappa=0.3
    no_move=0; no_improve=0; exact_calls=0; last_exact=t0
    max_exact = 800 if n>4096 else 2000
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        elapsed=time.monotonic()-t0
        frac = min(1.0, elapsed/max(1e-6,time_limit))
        # active check: if cur_score not finite, reset
        if not np.isfinite(cur_score): p=best_fast_p; Fp,c=compute_fp_conv(p,size,n); cur_score=...
        # Adaptive kappa
        kappa = 0.02 + 0.35 * (1.0 - frac)  # 0.37 early to0.02
        # compute q
        q = n * c
        maxq = float(np.max(q))
        # if fatal? 
        w, maxq = softmax_weights(q, kappa)
        h = weighted_overlap_from_fp(w, Fp, size, n)
        m = float(np.dot(p, h))
        if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 1e-300:
            # perturb
            ...
        hn = h / m
        # weighted std
        dev = hn - 1.0
        var = float(np.dot(p, dev*dev))
        sd = np.sqrt(max(var,0.0))
        # ideal beta
        target = 0.25 + 0.35*(1.0-frac)  # early0.6 late0.25
        if sd > 1e-8:
            ideal = target/sd
            ideal = min(3.0, max(0.02, ideal))
            beta = 0.75*beta + 0.25*ideal
        else:
            beta = min(3.0, max(0.02, beta*1.1))
        temperature = (0.008 if frac < 0.8 else 0.0005) * (1.0-frac)**2 + 1e-8
        # line search
        b = min(2.0, max(0.02, beta))
        accepted = False
        best_trial = None
        for _ in range(3):
            res = trial_update(p, hn, b, size, n)
            if res is not None:
                np2,c2,Fp2,s2=res
                if s2 < best_fast - 1e-10 or s2 < cur_score + temperature:
                    accepted=True
                    p=np2; c=c2; Fp=Fp2; cur_score=s2
                    if s2 < best_fast:
                        best_fast=s2; best_fast_p=np2.copy(); no_improve=0
                    else:
                        no_improve += 1  # accepted worsening
                    break
                else:
                    if best_trial is None or s2 < best_trial[3]:
                        best_trial=(np2,c2,Fp2,s2)
            b *= 0.5
        if not accepted:
            # accept best trial if not too bad
            if best_trial is not None and best_trial[3] < cur_score + 5.0*temperature + 1e-8:
                p,c,Fp,cur_score = best_trial
                if cur_score < best_fast: ...
                no_improve +=1; no_move +=? 
                accepted=True
            else:
                no_move += 1
                # reduce beta
                beta *= 0.75
        if not accepted:
            # perturbation if stuck
            if no_move > 40 or (no_improve > 150 and it > 200):
                if rng.random() < 0.7:
                    base = best_fast_p
                else:
                    base = p
                # perturb
                sigma = 0.15 + 0.25*rng.random()
                logp = np.log(base + 1e-300)
                noise = rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
                # smooth noise to avoid high freq? Use low_pass? simple:
                # noise cumulative? 
                p2 = np.exp(logp + noise)
                p2 = clean_sequence(p2, 1.0)
                Fp,c = compute_fp_conv(p2,size,n)
                cur_score = 2*n*c.max()
                p = p2
                beta = max(0.2, beta)
                no_move=0; no_improve=0
                # If p2 score much worse than best_fast plus 0.05, maybe try best_fast param instead?
                if not np.isfinite(cur_score) or cur_score > best_fast + 0.05:
                    p = best_fast_p.copy(); Fp,c=...; cur_score=best_fast
                    # still stuck? try smaller lambda later
        # exact eval
        now=time.monotonic()
        if (now - last_exact > 0.25 and exact_calls < max_exact and (best_fast < best_exact - 1e-7 or best_fast < cur_score - 1e-7 or it % 50 ==0)):
            # evaluate best_fast_p or current whichever fast lower?
            check = best_fast_p if best_fast <= cur_score else p
            try:
                ex = float(evaluate_sequence(check.tolist()))
                exact_calls +=1
                last_exact = now
                if np.isfinite(ex):
                    if ex < best_exact:
                        best_exact=ex; best_exact_p=check.copy()
                        if np.isfinite(best_fast_p) and best_fast < best_fast? print
                        print(f"[{label}] exact={ex:.8f} fast={best_fast:.8f} it={it} n={n}")
            except Exception:
                pass
        # If exact budget exhausted, reduce frequency
        # If time close to deadline, break after exact final
        it +=1
    # final evaluate best_fast_p and best_exact_p
    for cand in (best_fast_p, best_exact_p):
       try: ex=evaluate_sequence(cand.tolist()); update
       except
    return best_exact_p, best_exact
```

def make_start_pool(n, parent, shape_low, seed):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    starts=[]
    if parent is not None:
       starts.append(upsample_sequence(parent,n))

       for alpha in [0.8,1.5,2.5]? But only first few.

### Let's write `make_start_pool` with no lambdas
```
def make_start_pool(n, parent, shape_low, seed):
    out=[]
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # parent
    if parent is not None:
        base = upsample_sequence(parent, n)
        if base is not None:
            out.append(base)
            t = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
            # multiplicative ramps
            for alpha in (0.8,1.5,2.2, -0.8, -1.5, -2.2):
                arr = base * np.exp(alpha*(2.0*t -1.0))
                arr = clean_sequence(arr,1.0)
                out.append(arr)
    if shape_low is not None:
        out.append(upsample_sequence(shape_low,n))
    # random
    out.append(random_smooth_density(n, seed+101, 1.0, 8))
    out.append(random_smooth_density(n, seed+202, 1.5, 14))
    out.append(ramp_density(n, 1.2))
    # maybe gaussian two bumps
    # create a pair of gaussian asymmetry:
    t = np.linspace(0,1,n)
    for (mu1,sig1,mu2,sig2) in [(0.25,0.12,0.7,0.20),(0.35,0.18,0.80,0.10),(0.15,0.20,0.6,0.15)]:
        arr = np.exp(-0.5*((t-mu1)/sig1)**2) + 0.6*np.exp(-0.5*((t-mu2)/sig2)**2)
        out.append(clean_sequence(arr,1.0))
    # deduplicate? not necessary. remove None, ensure finite
    cleaned=[]
    for a in out:
       if a is None: continue
       a=clean_sequence(a,1.0)
       if a is None: continue
       if not np.isfinite(a).all(): continue
       cleaned.append(a)
    return cleaned
```
For parent, multiplicative ramps preserve relative shape plus asymmetry. Many starts 6+; sorting and selecting top 4, okay.

Memory fine.

```
def smooth_random_noise(n, rng, strength=1.0, modes=8):
    theta = rng.standard_normal(n)
    F=np.fft.rfft(theta)
    keep=min(len(F), modes+1); F[keep:]=0; s=irfft...
    s = (s-s.mean())/(std+1e-8); return strength*s
```
Then perturbation:
```
noise = smooth_random_noise(n, rng, sigma, 8)
p2 = np.exp(logp + noise)
```
This avoids high-frequency. Good.

Top-level `smooth_random_noise` uses randomness; no closure.

Also for random smooth density start, use similar.

Let's draft optimize_one in final code mentally:

```
def optimize_one(p0, seed=0, time_limit=60.0, external_best_p=None, extra_starts=None, label=""):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    time_limit = max(1.0, float(time_limit))
    deadline = t0 + time_limit
    p = clean_sequence(p0, 1.0)
    if p is None: return None, np.inf
    n = int(p.size)
    if n < 2:
       # exact
    size=1
    while size < 3*n - 2: size <<=1
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    extra_idx=0
    # init Fp,c
    Fp,c = compute_fp_conv(p,size,n)
    cur_score = 2.0 * n * float(np.max(c))
    best_fast = cur_score
    best_fast_p = p.copy()
    best_exact = np.inf; best_exact_p = p.copy()
    exact_calls=0; last_exact = t0 - 1.0
    exact_interval = 0.75 if n > 3000 else 0.35
    max_exact = 300 if n > 3000 else 1000
    last_eval_fast = best_fast
    # external best
    if external_best_p is not None:
       ebp = clean_sequence(external_best_p,1.0)
       if ebp is not None and ebp.size == n:
           f = fast_score(ebp)
           if np.isfinite(f) and f < best_fast:
              best_fast=f; best_fast_p=ebp.copy()
           if time_limit > 5.0:
              try: ex=evaluate_sequence(ebp.tolist()); update best_exact; last_eval_fast=best_fast
       # set initial p maybe? Keep p0, but if external much lower and p0 very bad, maybe set p=best_external for search? We want diversity; keep p0 but exact best held.
    # initial exact of p0 if time
    if time_limit > 2.0:
       try: ex=evaluate_sequence(p.tolist()); update
    beta=0.6; no_move=0; no_improve=0; it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       elapsed=time.monotonic()-t0; frac=... 
       if not finite cur_score: reset to best_fast
       # if current score much worse than best_fast and no_improve? maybe reset from best quickly
       if cur_score > best_fast + 0.05 and no_improve > 20:
           p=best_fast_p.copy(); Fp,c=...; cur_score=best_fast; no_improve=0; no_move=0; beta=0.4
       q = n * c
       # ensure no overflow; if c max finite
       kappa = 0.02 + 0.35 * (1.0 - frac)
       w, maxq = softmax_weights(q, kappa)
       h = weighted_overlap_from_fp(w, Fp, size, n)
       m = float(np.dot(p,h))
       if m < 1e-300 or not finite:
           # reset
           p=best_fast_p.copy(); Fp,c=compute...
           continue
       hn = h/m
       dev0=hn-1.0
       var = float(np.dot(p, dev0*dev0))
       sd = np.sqrt(max(var,0.0))
       target = 0.25 + 0.35*(1.0-frac)
       if sd > 1e-8:
           ideal=target/sd
           ideal = min(2.5, max(0.02, ideal))
           beta=0.75*beta+0.25*ideal
       else:
           beta=min(2.5, max(0.02, beta*1.05))
       temperature = 0.015 * (1.0 - frac) * (1.0 - frac) + 1e-8
       b = min(2.0, max(0.02, beta))
       accepted=False
       best_trial=None
       for _ in range(3):
           res=trial_update(p, hn, b, size, n)
           if res is None: b*=0.5; continue
           np2,c2,Fp2,s2=res
           if s2 < best_fast - 1e-10 or s2 < cur_score + temperature:
               accepted=True
               p=np2; c=c2; Fp=Fp2; cur_score=s2
               if s2 < best_fast:
                   best_fast=s2; best_fast_p=np2.copy(); no_improve=0
               else:
                   no_improve += 1
               break
           if best_trial is None or s2 < best_trial[3]:
               best_trial=(np2,c2,Fp2,s2)
           b *= 0.5
       if not accepted:
           if best_trial is not None and best_trial[3] < cur_score + 5.0*temperature + 1e-8:
               p,c,Fp,cur_score = best_trial
               if cur_score < best_fast:
                   best_fast=cur_score; best_fast_p=p.copy(); no_improve=0
               else:
                   no_improve += 1
               accepted=True
           else:
               no_move += 1
               beta *= 0.75
       if not accepted:
           if no_move >= 30 or (no_improve >= 200 and it > 100):
               if extra_starts is not None and len(extra_starts) > 0 and rng.random() < 0.25:
                   base = extra_starts[extra_idx % len(extra_starts)]
                   extra_idx += 1
                   base = clean_sequence(base, 1.0)
                   if base is None or base.size != n:
                       base = best_fast_p
               else:
                   base = best_fast_p if rng.random() < 0.75 else p
               sigma = 0.15 + 0.30 * rng.random()
               noise = smooth_random_noise(n, rng, sigma, 8)
               p2 = np.exp(np.log(base + 1e-300) + noise)
               p2 = clean_sequence(p2, 1.0)
               if p2 is None:
                   p2 = base.copy()
               Fp,c = compute_fp_conv(p2,size,n)
               s2=2*n*max(c)
               if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_fast + 0.05:
                   p=p2; cur_score=s2
               else:
                   p=best_fast_p.copy(); Fp,c=...; cur_score=best_fast
               beta = max(0.25, beta)
               no_move=0; no_improve=0
       # exact eval
       now=time.monotonic()
       if exact_calls < max_exact and (now - last_exact > exact_interval or (best_fast < last_eval_fast - 2e-6 and now - last_exact > 0.1)):
           check = best_fast_p
           if cur_score < best_fast - 1e-12:
               check = p
           eval_fast = best_fast
           if cur_score < best_fast - 1e-12: eval_fast=cur_score
           try:
               ex=float(evaluate_sequence(check.tolist()))
               exact_calls +=1
               last_exact=now; last_eval_fast=eval_fast
               if np.isfinite(ex):
                   if ex < best_exact:
                       best_exact=ex; best_exact_p=check.copy()
                       print(f"[{label}] exact improved {ex:.8f} fast {best_fast:.8f} n={n} it={it}")
           except Exception:
               pass
       it += 1
       # Near deadline, break after final exact maybe
       if deadline - time.monotonic() < 1.5:
           break
    # final exact of best_fast_p and best_exact_p
    for cand in (best_fast_p, best_exact_p):
       try:
           ex=float(evaluate_sequence(cand.tolist()))
           if np.isfinite(ex) and ex < best_exact:
               best_exact=ex; best_exact_p=cand.copy()
       except Exception:
           pass
    if not np.isfinite(best_exact):
       return best_fast_p, best_fast  # maybe fast score; propose will exact eval
    return best_exact_p, best_exact
```

`smooth_random_noise` top-level:
```
def smooth_random_noise(n, rng, strength=1.0, modes=8):
    theta = rng.standard_normal(n)
    F = np.fft.rfft(theta)
    keep = min(len(F), modes + 1)
    F[keep:] = 0.0
    s = np.fft.irfft(F, n)
    s -= s.mean()
    sd = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(s*s)))
    if sd < 1e-15:
       return np.zeros(n)
    return strength * s / sd
```
If strength 0? okay.

```
def get_parent_sequence():
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.ndim !=1: arr=arr.ravel()
    if arr.size==0: return None
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr,0.0)
    if arr.sum() <=0: return None
    return clean_sequence(arr,1.0)
```

Implement top-level `cross_y_refine`:
```
def cross_y_refine(p, Fp, size, n, max_iters=60, kappa=0.15, beta=0.7):
    y = p.copy()
    Fy = np.fft.rfft(y, size)
    for it in range(max_iters):
        cross = np.fft.irfft(Fp * Fy, size)[:2*n-1]
        cross = np.maximum(cross,0.0)
        qc = n * cross
        w,_=softmax_weights(qc,kappa)
        h = weighted_overlap_from_fp(w, Fp, size, n)
        m = float(np.dot(y,h))
        if m <= 0 or not finite: break
        hn = h / m
        factor = np.exp(-beta * np.clip(hn-1.0, -50,50))
        factor = np.clip(factor,1e-12,1e6)
        y = y * factor
        s = y.sum()
        if s<=0: y=p.copy(); break
        y /= s
        Fy = np.fft.rfft(y, size)
    return y
```

       Fp2,c2=...; s2=...

```

```
def cross_y_refine(p, Fp, size, n, max_iters=25, kappa=0.12, beta=0.8):
    y=p.copy()
    Fy=np.fft.rfft(y,size)
    for _ in range(max_iters):
       cross=np.fft.irfft(Fp*Fy,size)[:2*n-1]
       cross=np.maximum(cross,0.0)
       if cross.size==0: break
       qc=n*cross
       w,_=softmax_weights(qc,kappa)
       h=weighted_overlap_from_fp(w,Fp,size,n)
       m=float(np.dot(y,h))
       if m <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(m): break
       hn=h/m
       factor=np.exp(-beta*np.clip(hn-1.0,-50,50))
       factor=np.clip(factor,1e-12,1e6)
       y=y*factor
       s=float(np.sum(y))
       if s <= 0 or not finite: y=p.copy(); break
       y /= s
       Fy=np.fft.rfft(y,size)
    return y
```
This uses Fp for p. Good.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, time_limit_total=2400, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(30.0, float(time_limit_total) - 45.0)
    deadline = t0 + budget
    best_p = None
    best_score = np.inf
    parent = get_parent_sequence()
    # Evaluate parent
    if parent is not None:
        try:
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(parent.tolist()))
            if np.isfinite(sc): best_score=sc; best_p=parent.copy()
        except Exception: pass
    # shape search
    shape_low=None
    rem = remaining_time(deadline)
    if HAVE_SCIPY and rem > 140:
        shape_time = min(70.0, rem - 80.0)
        if shape_time > 20.0:
            shape_low = shape_search(512, 12, seed=31337, time_limit=shape_time)
            if shape_low is not None:
                fs = fast_score(shape_low)
                print("shape fast:", fs)
                try:
                    ex = float(evaluate_sequence(shape_low.tolist()))
                    if np.isfinite(ex) and ex < best_score:
                        best_score=ex; best_p=shape_low.copy()
                except Exception: pass
    # main length 4096
    n_main = 4096
    rem = remaining_time(deadline)
    if rem > 60:
        starts = make_start_pool(n_main, parent, shape_low, int(seed)+1)
        scored = score_and_sort_starts(starts)
        if scored:
            total_main = min(1850.0, rem)
            counts = min(4, len(scored))
            raw_weights = [0.55,0.25,0.12,0.08][:counts]
            sumw = float(sum(raw_weights))
            weights = [w / sumw for w in raw_weights]
            for i in range(counts):
                rem = remaining_time(deadline)
                if rem < 25: break
                alloc = total_main * weights[i]
                # if later runs have time, allow but not exceed rem-30
                alloc = min(alloc, rem - 20.0)
                if alloc < 10: continue
                external = None
                if best_p is not None:
                    external = upsample_sequence(best_p, n_main)
                extras = [scored[j][1] for j in range(i+1, counts)]
                loc_p, loc_score = optimize_one(scored[i][1], seed=int(seed)+100+7*i, time_limit=alloc, external_best_p=external, extra_starts=extras, label=f"main{i}")
                if loc_p is not None and np.isfinite(loc_score) and loc_score < best_score:
                    best_score = loc_score; best_p = loc_p.copy()
    # alt length
    n_alt = None
    if parent is not None and len(parent) >= 128:
        n_alt = len(parent)
    else:
        n_alt = 2048
    rem = remaining_time(deadline)
    if rem > 150:
        total_alt = min(500.0, rem)
        starts_alt = make_start_pool(n_alt, parent, shape_low, int(seed)+500)
        scored_alt = score_and_sort_starts(starts_alt)
        if scored_alt:
            counts_alt = min(2, len(scored_alt))
            weights_alt = [0.6,0.4][:counts_alt]
            sumw = float(sum(weights_alt)); weights_alt=[w/sumw for w in weights_alt]
            for i in range(counts_alt):
                rem = remaining_time(deadline)
                if rem < 25: break
                alloc = total_alt * weights_alt[i]
                alloc = min(alloc, rem-20.0)
                if alloc < 10: continue
                external = None
                if best_p is not None:
                    external = upsample_sequence(best_p, n_alt)
                extras = [scored_alt[j][1] for j in range(i+1, counts_alt)]
                loc_p, loc_score = optimize_one(scored_alt[i][1], seed=int(seed)+700+7*i, time_limit=alloc, external_best_p=external, extra_starts=extras, label=f"alt{i}")
                if loc_p is not None and np.isfinite(loc_score) and loc_score < best_score:
                    best_score=loc_score; best_p=loc_p.copy()
    # final refinement
    rem = remaining_time(deadline)
    if best_p is not None and rem > 90:
        n_fin = len(best_p)
        extras = []
        extras.append(random_smooth_density(n_fin, int(seed)+1000, 0.3, 6))
        extras.append(ramp_density(n_fin, 1.0))
        alloc = min(300.0, rem - 30.0)
        loc_p, loc_score = optimize_one(best_p, seed=int(seed)+1234, time_limit=alloc, external_best_p=best_p, extra_starts=extras, label="final")
        if loc_p is not None and np.isfinite(loc_score) and loc_score < best_score:
             best_score=loc_score; best_p=loc_p.copy()
    # Final exact eval
    if best_p is None:
        best_p = ramp_density(512, 0.0)
    try:
        final_score = float(evaluate_sequence(best_p.tolist()))
        if np.isfinite(final_score):
            best_score = final_score
    except Exception:
        pass
    print("Final best score:", best_score, "length:", len(best_p))
    return [float(v) for v in best_p]
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative sequences minimizing
    2 * n * max(conv(a, a)) / sum(a)^2
using:
  1. several diversified initial candidates (parent interpolation, smooth
     randoms, ramp/gaussian mixes, low-dimensional shape search),
  2. a multiplicative subgradient / entropic-max optimizer on p = a / sum(a),
  3. an auxiliary SOTA-style cross-convolution refinement when local search
     stagnates,
  4. periodic exact evaluation and final validation.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from operator import itemgetter

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _SP_MINIMIZE
    HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _SP_MINIMIZE = None
    HAVE_SCIPY = False


def remaining_time(deadline):
    return max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())


def clean_sequence(a, target_sum=1.0):
    """Convert to a finite, nonnegative, normalized 1D float64 array."""
    if a is None:
        return None
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    a = np.minimum(a, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(a.shape, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(a.sum())
    a = (a / s) * target_sum
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(a.shape, target_sum / max(1, a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s * target_sum


def fft_self_conv(a):
    """Fast full self-convolution via FFT."""
    n = int(len(a))
    if n <= 1:
        return a * a
    size = 1
    while size < 2 * n - 1:
        size <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)[:2 * n - 1]
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def fast_score(a):
    """Fast FFT-based score for sum(a)=1; exact up to tiny FFT error."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(len(a))
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    c = fft_self_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))


def upsample_sequence(a, n):
    """Linearly resample a normalized sequence to length n."""
    if a is None or n <= 0:
        return None
    a = clean_sequence(a, 1.0)
    if a is None:
        return None
    n = int(n)
    if a.size == n:
        return a
    if a.size == 1:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    xp = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    xq = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    b = np.interp(xq, xp, a)
    return clean_sequence(b, 1.0)


def ramp_density(n, alpha=1.0):
    """A smooth exponential ramp start."""
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    p = np.exp(alpha * (2.0 * t - 1.0))
    return clean_sequence(p, 1.0)


def smooth_random_noise(n, rng, strength=1.0, modes=8):
    """Low-pass random noise for rank-one multiplicitive perturbations."""
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if n <= 1:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = rng.standard_normal(n)
    F = np.fft.rfft(theta)
    keep = min(len(F), max(1, int(modes) + 1))
    F[keep:] = 0.0
    s = np.fft.irfft(F, n)
    s -= s.mean()
    sd = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(s * s)))
    if sd < 1e-15:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return strength * s / sd


def random_smooth_density(n, seed=0, strength=1.0, low_modes=8):
    """Positive smooth random density."""
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    s = smooth_random_noise(n, rng, strength, low_modes)
    p = np.exp(s)
    return clean_sequence(p, 1.0)


def shape_objective(v, n_low, M, xk, xq):
    """
    Objective for a piecewise-linear nonnegative density represented by
    logarithmic knot values v.
    """
    try:
        raw = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
        if raw.size != M:
            raw = raw.reshape(-1)
        if raw.size != M or not np.isfinite(raw).all():
            return 10.0
        raw = np.clip(raw, -6.0, 6.0)
        vals = np.exp(raw)
        if vals.sum() <= 0.0:
            return 10.0
        dens = np.interp(xq, xk, vals)
        dens = np.maximum(dens, 0.0)
        s = float(dens.sum())
        if s <= 0.0:
            return 10.0
        dens /= s
        c = fft_self_conv(dens)
        if c.size == 0:
            return 10.0
        return float(2.0 * n_low * np.max(c))
    except Exception:
        return 10.0


def shape_search(n_low, M, seed=1, time_limit=45.0):
    """
    Low-dimensional global-shape search using piecewise-linear densities.
    Returns a normalized density of length n_low, or None.
    """
    if not HAVE_SCIPY:
        return None
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    n_low = int(max(64, n_low))
    M = int(max(4, min(M, n_low)))
    xk = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, M)
    xq = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_low)

    starts = []
    starts.append(np.zeros(M, dtype=np.float64))
    starts.append(np.linspace(-1.4, 1.6, M, dtype=np.float64))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    starts.append(0.7 * rng.standard_normal(M))

    best_v = None
    best_s = np.inf
    opts = {"maxiter": 700, "xatol": 0.05, "fatol": 1e-7}

    for x0 in starts:
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > max(5.0, time_limit):
            break
        x0 = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if x0.size != M:
            continue
        s0 = shape_objective(x0, n_low, M, xk, xq)
        if np.isfinite(s0) and s0 < best_s:
            best_s = s0
            best_v = x0.copy()
        try:
            res = _SP_MINIMIZE(
                shape_objective,
                x0,
                args=(n_low, M, xk, xq),
                method="Nelder-Mead",
                options=opts,
            )
        except Exception:
            continue
        if res is None:
            continue
        x = getattr(res, "x", x0)
        try:
            x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        except Exception:
            continue
        if x.size != M:
            continue
        s = shape_objective(x, n_low, M, xk, xq)
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_s:
            best_s = s
            best_v = x.copy()

    if best_v is None:
        return None

    # small random polish
    for _ in range(30):
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > max(10.0, time_limit):
            break
        v = np.clip(best_v + 0.18 * rng.standard_normal(best_v.size), -6.0, 6.0)
        s = shape_objective(v, n_low, M, xk, xq)
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_s:
            best_s = s
            best_v = v.copy()

    vals = np.exp(np.clip(best_v, -6.0, 6.0))
    dens = np.interp(xq, xk, vals)
    return clean_sequence(dens, 1.0)


def compute_fp_conv(p, size, n):
    """Return rfft(p, size) and the full self-convolution of p."""
    Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(Fp * Fp, size)[:2 * n - 1]
    return Fp, np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def weighted_overlap_from_fp(w, Fp, size, n):
    """
    h_i = sum_k w_k p_{k-i}, where Fp = rfft(p, size).
    """
    Lc = 2 * n - 1
    wr = w[::-1]
    Fw = np.fft.rfft(wr, size)
    h_full = np.fft.irfft(Fw * Fp, size)
    h = h_full[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
    return np.maximum(h, 0.0)


def softmax_weights(q, kappa):
    """Stable softmax weights over coefficients q."""
    if kappa <= 1e-12:
        w = np.zeros_like(q, dtype=np.float64)
        idx = int(np.argmax(q))
        w[idx] = 1.0
        return w, float(q[idx])
    maxq = float(np.max(q))
    e = np.exp((q - maxq) / kappa)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(q, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(q))] = 1.0
        return w, maxq
    return e / s, maxq


def trial_update(p, hn, beta, size, n):
    """One multiplicative gradient step and its fast score."""
    dev = hn - 1.0
    dev = np.clip(dev, -50.0, 50.0)
    factor = np.exp(-beta * dev)
    factor = np.clip(factor, 1e-12, 1e6)
    np2 = p * factor
    s = float(np.sum(np2))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    np2 = np2 / s
    Fp2 = np.fft.rfft(np2, size)
    c2 = np.maximum(np.fft.irfft(Fp2 * Fp2, size)[:2 * n - 1], 0.0)
    score2 = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c2))
    if not np.isfinite(score2):
        return None
    return np2, c2, Fp2, score2


def cross_y_refine(p, Fp, size, n, max_iters=25, kappa=0.12, beta=0.8):
    """
    SOTA-inspired auxiliary step:
    for fixed p, approximately minimize max_k (p*y)_k over y >= 0, sum y = 1.
    """
    y = p.copy()
    Fy = np.fft.rfft(y, size)
    for _ in range(max(1, int(max_iters))):
        cross = np.maximum(np.fft.irfft(Fp * Fy, size)[:2 * n - 1], 0.0)
        if cross.size == 0:
            break
        qc = n * cross
        w, _ = softmax_weights(qc, kappa)
        h = weighted_overlap_from_fp(w, Fp, size, n)
        m = float(np.dot(y, h))
        if m <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(m):
            break
        hn = h / m
        factor = np.exp(-beta * np.clip(hn - 1.0, -50.0, 50.0))
        factor = np.clip(factor, 1e-12, 1e6)
        y = y * factor
        s = float(np.sum(y))
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            y = p.copy()
            break
        y = y / s
        Fy = np.fft.rfft(y, size)
    return y


def optimize_one(p0, seed=0, time_limit=60.0, external_best_p=None,
                 extra_starts=None, label=""):
    """
    Local global search from p0 (same length n) for time_limit seconds.
    Returns (best_sequence, best_known_score).
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    time_limit = max(1.0, float(time_limit))
    deadline = t0 + time_limit

    p = clean_sequence(p0, 1.0)
    if p is None:
        return None, np.inf
    n = int(p.size)
    if n < 2:
        try:
            return p, float(evaluate_sequence(p.tolist()))
        except Exception:
            return p, 2.0

    size = 1
    while size < 3 * n - 2:
        size <<= 1

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    extra_idx = 0

    Fp, c = compute_fp_conv(p, size, n)
    cur_score = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))
    best_fast = cur_score
    best_fast_p = p.copy()

    best_exact = np.inf
    best_exact_p = p.copy()

    exact_calls = 0
    exact_interval = 0.75 if n > 3000 else 0.35
    max_exact = 350 if n > 3000 else 1200
    last_exact = t0 - 1.0
    last_eval_fast = best_fast

    if external_best_p is not None:
        ebp = clean_sequence(external_best_p, 1.0)
        if ebp is not None and ebp.size == n:
            f = fast_score(ebp)
            if np.isfinite(f) and f < best_fast:
                best_fast = f
                best_fast_p = ebp.copy()
            if time_limit > 5.0:
                try:
                    ex = float(evaluate_sequence(ebp.tolist()))
                    if np.isfinite(ex):
                        best_exact = ex
                        best_exact_p = ebp.copy()
                        last_eval_fast = best_fast
                        last_exact = time.monotonic()
                        exact_calls += 1
                except Exception:
                    pass

    if time_limit > 2.0:
        try:
            ex = float(evaluate_sequence(p.tolist()))
            if np.isfinite(ex):
                best_exact = min(best_exact, ex)
                if ex < best_exact:
                    best_exact = ex
                    best_exact_p = p.copy()
                last_eval_fast = best_fast
                last_exact = time.monotonic()
                exact_calls += 1
        except Exception:
            pass

    beta = 0.6
    no_move = 0
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
        frac = min(1.0, elapsed / max(1e-9, time_limit))

        if (not np.isfinite(cur_score)) or (not np.isfinite(best_fast)):
            p = best_fast_p
            Fp, c = compute_fp_conv(p, size, n)
            cur_score = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))
            best_fast = cur_score

        if cur_score > best_fast + 0.05 and no_improve > 25:
            p = best_fast_p.copy()
            Fp, c = compute_fp_conv(p, size, n)
            cur_score = best_fast
            no_move = 0
            no_improve = 0
            beta = 0.45

        q = n * c
        kappa = 0.012 + 0.30 * (1.0 - frac)
        w, _ = softmax_weights(q, kappa)
        h = weighted_overlap_from_fp(w, Fp, size, n)
        m = float(np.dot(p, h))
        if m <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(m):
            p = best_fast_p.copy()
            Fp, c = compute_fp_conv(p, size, n)
            cur_score = best_fast
            no_move += 1
            continue

        hn = h / m
        dev = hn - 1.0
        var = float(np.dot(p, dev * dev))
        sd = float(np.sqrt(max(0.0, var)))
        target = 0.15 + 0.45 * (1.0 - frac)
        if sd > 1e-8:
            ideal = target / sd
            ideal = min(2.5, max(0.02, ideal))
            beta = 0.75 * beta + 0.25 * ideal
        else:
            beta = min(2.5, max(0.02, 1.05 * beta))

        temperature = 0.018 * (1.0 - frac) * (1.0 - frac) + 1e-8
        b = min(2.0, max(0.02, beta))
        accepted = False
        best_trial = None

        for _ in range(3):
            res = trial_update(p, hn, b, size, n)
            if res is None:
                b *= 0.5
                continue
            np2, c2, Fp2, s2 = res
            if s2 < best_fast - 1e-10 or s2 < cur_score + temperature:
                accepted = True
                p = np2
                c = c2
                Fp = Fp2
                cur_score = s2
                if s2 < best_fast:
                    best_fast = s2
                    best_fast_p = np2.copy()
                    no_improve = 0
                else:
                    no_improve += 1
                if b < 0.75 * beta:
                    beta = max(0.02, 0.5 * beta)
                elif no_improve == 0:
                    beta = min(2.5, 1.05 * beta)
                break
            if best_trial is None or s2 < best_trial[3]:
                best_trial = (np2, c2, Fp2, s2)
            b *= 0.5

        if not accepted:
            if best_trial is not None and best_trial[3] < cur_score + 5.0 * temperature + 1e-8:
                p, c, Fp, cur_score = best_trial
                if cur_score < best_fast:
                    best_fast = cur_score
                    best_fast_p = p.copy()
                    no_improve = 0
                else:
                    no_improve += 1
                accepted = True
            else:
                no_move += 1
                beta *= 0.75

        if not accepted:
            if no_move >= 30 or (no_improve >= 250 and it > 100):
                moved = False
                # SOTA-style cross-convolution escape.
                if deadline - time.monotonic() > 1.2 and rng.random() < 0.65:
                    y = cross_y_refine(p, Fp, size, n, max_iters=25,
                                       kappa=0.12, beta=0.8)
                    for alpha in (0.50, 0.30, 0.15, 0.06):
                        p2 = (1.0 - alpha) * p + alpha * y
                        p2 = clean_sequence(p2, 1.0)
                        if p2 is None:
                            continue
                        Fp2, c2 = compute_fp_conv(p2, size, n)
                        s2 = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c2))
                        if np.isfinite(s2) and (s2 < best_fast or s2 < cur_score + 0.012):
                            p = p2
                            Fp = Fp2
                            c = c2
                            cur_score = s2
                            if s2 < best_fast:
                                best_fast = s2
                                best_fast_p = p2.copy()
                                no_improve = 0
                            else:
                                no_improve += 1
                            moved = True
                            break

                # Random smooth escape.
                if not moved:
                    if extra_starts is not None and len(extra_starts) > 0 and rng.random() < 0.25:
                        base = extra_starts[extra_idx % len(extra_starts)]
                        extra_idx += 1
                        base = clean_sequence(base, 1.0)
                        if base is None or base.size != n:
                            base = best_fast_p
                    else:
                        base = best_fast_p if rng.random() < 0.75 else p

                    sigma = 0.15 + 0.30 * rng.random()
                    noise = smooth_random_noise(n, rng, sigma, 8)
                    p2 = np.exp(np.log(base + 1e-300) + noise)
                    p2 = clean_sequence(p2, 1.0)
                    if p2 is None:
                        p2 = base.copy()
                    Fp2, c2 = compute_fp_conv(p2, size, n)
                    s2 = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c2))
                    if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_fast + 0.05:
                        p = p2
                        Fp = Fp2
                        c = c2
                        cur_score = s2
                    else:
                        p = best_fast_p.copy()
                        Fp, c = compute_fp_conv(p, size, n)
                        cur_score = best_fast
                    beta = max(0.25, beta)

                no_move = 0
                if not moved:
                    no_improve = 0

        now = time.monotonic()
        if exact_calls < max_exact and (
            now - last_exact > exact_interval or
            (best_fast < last_eval_fast - 2e-6 and now - last_exact > 0.05)
        ):
            check = best_fast_p
            check_fast = best_fast
            if cur_score < best_fast - 1e-12:
                check = p
                check_fast = cur_score
            try:
                ex = float(evaluate_sequence(check.tolist()))
                exact_calls += 1
                last_exact = now
                last_eval_fast = check_fast
                if np.isfinite(ex) and ex < best_exact:
                    best_exact = ex
                    best_exact_p = check.copy()
                    print(f"[{label}] exact improved: {ex:.8f}, fast={best_fast:.8f}, n={n}, it={it}")
            except Exception:
                pass

        it += 1
        if deadline - time.monotonic() < 1.2:
            break

    # Final validation inside this run.
    for cand in (best_fast_p, best_exact_p):
        try:
            ex = float(evaluate_sequence(cand.tolist()))
            if np.isfinite(ex) and ex < best_exact:
                best_exact = ex
                best_exact_p = cand.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    if np.isfinite(best_exact) and best_exact_p is not None:
        return best_exact_p, best_exact
    return best_fast_p, best_fast


def make_start_pool(n, parent, shape_low, seed):
    """Create diverse starts of length n."""
    n = int(max(1, n))
    out = []

    if parent is not None:
        base = upsample_sequence(parent, n)
        if base is not None:
            out.append(base)
            t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
            for alpha in (0.8, 1.5, 2.2, -0.8, -1.5, -2.2):
                arr = base * np.exp(alpha * (2.0 * t - 1.0))
                out.append(clean_sequence(arr, 1.0))

    if shape_low is not None:
        out.append(upsample_sequence(shape_low, n))

    out.append(random_smooth_density(n, seed + 101, 1.0, 8))
    out.append(random_smooth_density(n, seed + 202, 1.5, 14))
    out.append(ramp_density(n, 1.2))

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    for mu1, sig1, mu2, sig2 in (
        (0.25, 0.12, 0.70, 0.20),
        (0.35, 0.18, 0.80, 0.10),
        (0.15, 0.20, 0.60, 0.15),
    ):
        arr = (
            np.exp(-0.5 * ((t - mu1) / sig1) ** 2)
            + 0.65 * np.exp(-0.5 * ((t - mu2) / sig2) ** 2)
        )
        out.append(clean_sequence(arr, 1.0))

    cleaned = []
    for a in out:
        a = clean_sequence(a, 1.0)
        if a is None:
            continue
        if not np.isfinite(a).all():
            continue
        cleaned.append(a)
    return cleaned


def score_and_sort_starts(starts):
    """Return (fast_score, sequence) sorted ascending."""
    scored = []
    for a in starts:
        a = clean_sequence(a, 1.0)
        if a is None:
            continue
        sc = fast_score(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, a))
    scored.sort(key=itemgetter(0))
    return scored


def get_parent_sequence():
    """Read the global previous best if available."""
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.ndim != 1:
        arr = arr.ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    if arr.sum() <= 0.0:
        return None
    return clean_sequence(arr, 1.0)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, time_limit_total=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Entry point. Searches over several lengths and repopulations, returning
    the best sequence found within the runtime budget.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        seed_val = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        seed_val = 42

    # Leave a safety margin for final evaluation and return.
    budget = max(60.0, float(time_limit_total) - 100.0)
    deadline = t0 + budget

    parent = get_parent_sequence()
    best_p = None
    best_score = np.inf

    if parent is not None:
        try:
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(parent.tolist()))
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                best_score = sc
                best_p = parent.copy()
                print(f"Initial parent exact score: {best_score:.8f}, n={len(parent)}")
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Quick low-dimensional global shape search.
    shape_low = None
    rem = remaining_time(deadline)
    if HAVE_SCIPY and rem > 150:
        shape_time = min(70.0, rem - 80.0)
        if shape_time > 20.0:
            shape_low = shape_search(512, 12, seed=31337, time_limit=shape_time)
            if shape_low is not None:
                fs = fast_score(shape_low)
                print(f"Shape search fast score: {fs:.8f}")
                try:
                    ex = float(evaluate_sequence(shape_low.tolist()))
                    if np.isfinite(ex) and ex < best_score:
                        best_score = ex
                        best_p = shape_low.copy()
                except Exception:
                    pass

    # Main high-resolution search.
    n_main = 4096
    rem = remaining_time(deadline)
    if rem > 60:
        starts = make_start_pool(n_main, parent, shape_low, seed_val + 1)
        scored = score_and_sort_starts(starts)
        if scored:
            total_main = min(1700.0, rem)
            counts = min(4, len(scored))
            raw_weights = [0.55, 0.25, 0.12, 0.08][:counts]
            sumw = float(sum(raw_weights))
            weights = [w / sumw for w in raw_weights]

            for i in range(counts):
                rem = remaining_time(deadline)
                if rem < 25:
                    break
                alloc = total_main * weights[i]
                alloc = min(alloc, rem - 20.0)
                if alloc < 10:
                    continue

                external = None
                if best_p is not None:
                    external = upsample_sequence(best_p, n_main)

                extras = [scored[j][1] for j in range(i + 1, counts)]
                loc_p, loc_score = optimize_one(
                    scored[i][1],
                    seed=seed_val + 100 + 7 * i,
                    time_limit=alloc,
                    external_best_p=external,
                    extra_starts=extras,
                    label=f"main{n_main}-{i}",
                )
                if loc_p is not None and np.isfinite(loc_score) and loc_score < best_score:
                    best_score = loc_score
                    best_p = loc_p.copy()
                    print(f"After main{i}: {best_score:.8f}, n={len(best_p)}")

    # Secondary search at parent length (or a reasonable backup length).
    if parent is not None and len(parent) >= 128:
        n_alt = len(parent)
    else:
        n_alt = 2048

    rem = remaining_time(deadline)
    if rem > 150:
        total_alt = min(400.0, rem)
        starts_alt = make_start_pool(n_alt, parent, shape_low, seed_val + 500)
        scored_alt = score_and_sort_starts(starts_alt)
        if scored_alt:
            counts_alt = min(2, len(scored_alt))
            weights_alt = [0.6, 0.4][:counts_alt]
            sumw = float(sum(weights_alt))
            weights_alt = [w / sumw for w in weights_alt]

            for i in range(counts_alt):
                rem = remaining_time(deadline)
                if rem < 25:
                    break
                alloc = total_alt * weights_alt[i]
                alloc = min(alloc, rem - 20.0)
                if alloc < 10:
                    continue

                external = None
                if best_p is not None:
                    external = upsample_sequence(best_p, n_alt)

                extras = [scored_alt[j][1] for j in range(i + 1, counts_alt)]
                loc_p, loc_score = optimize_one(
                    scored_alt[i][1],
                    seed=seed_val + 700 + 7 * i,
                    time_limit=alloc,
                    external_best_p=external,
                    extra_starts=extras,
                    label=f"alt{n_alt}-{i}",
                )
                if loc_p is not None and np.isfinite(loc_score) and loc_score < best_score:
                    best_score = loc_score
                    best_p = loc_p.copy()
                    print(f"After alt{i}: {best_score:.8f}, n={len(best_p)}")

    # Final refinement on the best sequence found so far.
    rem = remaining_time(deadline)
    if best_p is not None and rem > 90:
        n_fin = len(best_p)
        extras = [
            random_smooth_density(n_fin, seed_val + 1000, 0.3, 6),
            ramp_density(n_fin, 1.0),
        ]
        alloc = min(220.0, rem - 30.0)
        loc_p, loc_score = optimize_one(
            best_p,
            seed=seed_val + 1234,
            time_limit=alloc,
            external_best_p=best_p,
            extra_starts=extras,
            label="final",
        )
        if loc_p is not None and np.isfinite(loc_score) and loc_score < best_score:
            best_score = loc_score
            best_p = loc_p.copy()

    if best_p is None:
        best_p = ramp_density(512, 0.0)

    # Final exact validation.
    try:
        final_score = float(evaluate_sequence(best_p.tolist()))
        if np.isfinite(final_score):
            best_score = final_score
    except Exception:
        pass

    print(f"Final best score: {best_score:.8f}, length={len(best_p)}")
    return [float(x) for x in best_p]
```
